# Area REL — Version 3 to colleagues (the staff release, Stage 6 of the master plan)

## COLLEAGUE RELEASE GATE (added 9 Oct)

Things that must be true before any colleague installs CE. No earlier gate list existed in this file; this is the first. Each item names the carpark entry. Tim's call on every item that says so.

1. **Tim is the CE administrator before any colleague installs:** run Manager > Set up CE administrator on Tim's PC with the office folder synced and check the shared admin list file exists in the office policies folder (BUG-476). Nobody else writes it. **DONE on Tim's PC, SEEN by Codex 10 Oct 00:31 (W53 PASS 3 of 3): the shared admin list holds one entry, Tim's Windows login; Tim has not confirmed it yet; arrival on other PCs not shown.**
2. **Roll Back is hidden** until the installer's older-version path is proven (BUG-477, BUG-441).
3. **The real update route a colleague can follow is built, or the update messages are reworded** so they stop naming an installer nobody can run (BUG-478).
4. **The old install scripts and builds in the office folder are moved to an OLD folder** (BUG-483; Tim's call, it is publishing).
5. **The Manager fault report and CEMANAGERDIAG must code the login, PC name and folders before any colleague uses them** (BUG-488). Until then colleagues are told not to press them.
6. **Publish Issue stamps only the sheets the drafter ticked (or a colleague issuing a set stamps every sheet in the job): decide and fix before any colleague issues a set** (BUG-495 S-01). Today it gives the typed revision and issue date to every Structural sheet, including sheets left out, which breaks Tim's 14 Sep ruling.
7. **The job record (wind class, soil) lives with the job, not under one PC's drawing path** (BUG-496 S-02). Today a colleague, a copy or a Save As starts with an empty record. Tim's call: keep it in the drawing, in the job folder, or say plainly it is for this PC only.
8. **The X-Civil and LandXML imports read the source drawing's units (or stop and ask): a millimetre drawing must never come in 1000 times too big without a word** (BUG-505 HUNT2-C-01). Today a millimetre X-Civil drawing can land 1000 times too far out and 1000 times too big with no message, because CE guesses the units from the first small pit or short pipe it finds and never reads the drawing's own UNITS. Not seen in AutoCAD yet.
9. **No blue anywhere a colleague sees or prints:** the old deep blue 1E5AA8 is replaced by gold, black and white/grey in windows, ribbon icons, help and the printed computations sheet (BUG-517, Tim INT-075). Tim said: 'ideally no blue please our colours are gold, black and white /grey'. Today the light theme of Civil, Manager and Structural windows, 406 of 410 light-ribbon icons and the help pages are that blue, and the Civil computations sheet prints the practice name and its rule line in blue onto paper and PDF. Not seen in AutoCAD yet.
10. **Markup and chat send nothing off the PC unless a Settings value that defaults OFF is on** (BUG-549, desk ruling enforcing Tim's 25 Sep rule): today the Structural Read Markup PDF command in its default Hybrid and Hand modes uploads the whole markup PDF, title block included, to OpenAI whenever an OpenAI key is set on the PC, and the markup chat also goes to OpenAI. Tim's rule (verified in his own words, UX-QA-05 'Yes, Claude only, page only (Recommended)' and 'Don't send; say so (Recommended)' when the title block cannot be blanked; decisions\2026-09-25_V3_PLAN_DISCUSSION_RULINGS.md line 772, 04_REFERENCE\structural\cards\STRUC-MARKUP-QA_CARD_2026-09-26.md): only the markup page image goes, only to Claude, never OpenAI, title block blanked, and a page whose title block cannot be blanked is NOT sent. On Tim's own PC no OpenAI key is set, so nothing leaves today. THE WHOLE FAMILY (off-PC calls audit 9 Oct, 17 routes, areas/release-v3-colleagues-OFF_PC_CALLS_2026-10-09.md): CEMARKUPPARSE, the CEMARKUP Parse button, CEMARKUPBENCH (every PDF in a folder and its subfolders), CEMARKUP Ask AI (sends the full drawing path) and the Enable MarkupAI switch that nothing reads are BUG-550; the Civil CDIMPORTXCIVIL AI extraction (up to 6 whole pages, Enter = Yes, no seat check) and CDASK Analyse markups (up to 5 pages, no blanking) are BUG-551; the rest of the audit is BUG-552 to BUG-557. Tim's PC has no OpenAI key set so nothing goes to OpenAI today, but it does use the CE Cloud proxy for the Civil AI paths (CE_CLOUD_URL and CE_CLOUD_AUTH are set).
11. **NO COLLEAGUE INSTALL, PILOT OR CESUPPORT HAND-OUT BEFORE STRUCTURAL 1.0.113 IS INSTALLED AND THE CIVIL PRIVACY TRAIN C-35 IS INSTALLED -- the Civil privacy train C-35 (#952 Civil AI uses the CE Cloud route only, #953 'AI features' master switch OFF by default, #954 fault-record upload switch OFF after its fix) is installed (desk condition, 9 Oct): 1.0.113 is the first Structural build with the markup upload fix (BUG-549, PR #949), and until then a PC that has an OpenAI key set could send a whole markup PDF off the PC. AND NEVER set OPENAI_API_KEY as a Windows environment variable on any PC that runs CE (Tim's PC has none, so nothing leaves it today).** The gate list is the authority; the DAY1 page and the pilot checklist repeat this line. **STATUS 10 Oct 06:16: Structural 1.0.113 (S-35, build 2e0cf385) AND Civil 3.0.44 / Manager 1.1.44 (C-35, build b7eed253) are INSTALLED on Tim's PC (built, Opus-signed, nine DLLs verified); the checks (AI-off messages, nothing sent, layout tab sends nothing) are still to be driven by the Claude driver pass; Tim has not confirmed anything in AutoCAD. Until that pass is green this item stays OPEN.**

Every successor release chat keeps these rules. A handoff that drops them is faulty.

**HOLIDAY QUEUE (plan/05, 29 Sep 2026): the ordered work while Tim is away is `areas/release-v3-colleagues-QUEUE.md` - read its sections 0-5 after this charter. TWO CHATS (Tim, 29 Sep ~17:15): the controller (this chat's successors, `build + release / 06` on) builds, tests, runs Codex and installs; Tim's desk `CE_ACAD / ideas / plan / 01` (`TIM_DESK.md`) takes everything Tim says. Charter items 3 and 10 are now done by the desk.** Tim, 29 Sep: "Everything first" (no colleague copy until all of V3 passes), Claude cap **90%** while he is away, **no colleague publishing until he is back** (`decisions/2026-09-28_HOLIDAY_AUTONOMOUS_RUN.md`, 29 Sep section).

1. **Usage first.** Before every batch, read both budgets:
   - **Claude:** `get_usage` (weekly all-models %, reset Fri 12:00 Melbourne).
   - **Codex:** `rate_limits.primary.used_percent` in the newest-written `~\.codex\sessions\**\rollout-*.jsonl` (reset shown as `resets_at`).
   - Pace line = % of week elapsed x 0.75 for Claude (25% kept for Tim). **While Tim is on holiday: x 0.90, 10% kept (Tim, 29 Sep).** Ahead of pace: one Sonnet builder, no Opus. Behind pace: up to 3-4 Sonnet builders.
   - Codex: a full AutoCAD pass costs ~7%. At least one a day; a second when the week's pace allows.
2. **Always building.** Any area, V3 plan and V3 UX plan together (`04_REFERENCE/ux-plan/V3_UX_PLAN.md` is part of V3). Builders run while Codex tests. Order: BUG-189 speed first, then open bugs in `BUG_CARPARK.md`, then the V3/UX queue.
3. **Tim's side messages never stop the work.** Classify, file it (bug, idea or plan item), give him a rough timeline or % to done, carry on.
4. **Bank for Codex.** Everything built since the last pass goes into ONE consolidated Codex AutoCAD pass a day. **Codex runner (moved 28 Sep out of an old chat's temp folder):** `C:\Lisp Files\_CODEX_RUNNER\` (not a repo). `codex_usage.sh` (usage), `daily_cap.sh explain` (today's cap), `queue3.sh name|worktree|model|effort|maxpct|cost` runs a brief `<name>.txt` + `common.txt`; results in `out\` and `queue.log`; reports land in `C:\Lisp Files\_TEST_COPIES_20260923\`.
5. **Installs on Tim's PC at the chat's discretion.** AutoCAD checked closed twice, no Codex run active, backup, read-back, undo named. **Colleague publishing needs Tim's word.** Merge = independent review + Codex pass.
6. **End goal:** a colleague-ready V3, with a Codex human-use/UX pass before it is offered to anyone.
7. **Speed and reliability are the framework** (`ENGINEERING_RULEBOOK.md`, section "Speed and reliability framework"). Every change is checked against it.
8. **Plan hygiene.** This file, `BUG_CARPARK.md`, `PENDING_TESTS.md` and the ideas file are updated in the same step as the work. Keep entries short so the next chat reads less. Context: delegate file-heavy reading to agents; keep the main chat for coordination.
9. **Learn.** Each batch ends by adding what went wrong or right to "Lessons" below (one line each), and the next batch reads them.
10. **Tell Tim:** brief, visual, real-world example. Save suggestions (better tools, online resources, upgrades, features) for when he is around; list them under "For Tim when he is around" below.
11. **TimOS:** `C:\TimOS\RULES.md` and `ROUTER.md` read every session; the overrides this charter creates are listed in the decision file.

### Lessons (append one line per batch; newest last)
1. 28 Sep: a builder's net10 Civil build needs the missing `CE_TRANSFORM_2027.arx` (no C++ toolset on this PC); a placeholder works for builds but is never shipped. Always read a DLL version back before trusting a build.
3. 28 Sep: the Opus review of the BUG-189 speed fix caught 4 real regressions that source-text contract tests could not (an unnamed Undo step from a document lock at idle, MLINE outlines recreated with new handles, link-data-only edits, a dirty list fed by other commands). Keep the independent review on anything touching reactors, Undo or caches, even under usage pressure.
2. 28 Sep: Manager has no file logger; timing written with `Trace` is invisible unless something listens. Check where a log line actually lands before relying on it.

4. 28 Sep ~20:20, Tim: "i didnt tell you to stop whilst codex was running". While Codex tests, keep 2-3 builders running within budget; never wait idle on a review or a Codex pass.

5. 28 Sep: a Codex AutoCAD pass cannot share the PC with Tim - it loses the window when he uses another program. Schedule passes for when he is off the PC (late evening / early morning / weekends).

6. 28 Sep: the 3.0.11 release branch bumped the Civil project version but not the repo's `Installer/PackageContents.xml` (still 3.0.10; test PackageContents_VersionMatchesCivilAssemblyVersion fails). The INSTALLED manifest reads AppVersion 3.0.11 (the installer stamps it), so nothing on Tim's PC is wrong. Every version bump: csproj AND PackageContents.xml, and run that test before installing.

7. 28 Sep: source-text tests that search for multi-line markers must normalise line endings - the combined release checkout is CRLF and a "
        }
    }
}" marker failed the install gate (fixed fc6d2e68). The gate did its job; the code was fine.

8. 29 Sep, Tim: "What do you keep checking in for? Just build, test, send to Codex, and repeat. And keep building whilst Codex is testing." Heartbeat is now every 2 h and SILENT unless something happened; message Tim only for results, installs, Codex outcomes, blockers or his decisions.

9. 29 Sep, Tim waiting with AutoCAD closed: "CAN IT NOT BE FASTER?" Build and verify the whole install payload (all lanes, Structural release candidate, natives staged) BEFORE asking Tim to close AutoCAD; the closed-AutoCAD window is only backup + copy + hash read-back (2-3 min).

10. 29 Sep, Tim: "YOU RUINED RC ... I WANTED YOU TO FIX ONE THING AND ITS CHANGED THE PROPERTIES COMPLETELY". A fix for one symptom must change only that symptom: diff the command's settings/properties before vs after and keep everything else byte-for-byte; reviews must check "what else changed" in drawing output, not just the bug.

11. 29 Sep (controller 06, first day): Claude weekly meter 95% (resets Fri 2 Oct 12:00), so only Week 0 work: timers, Codex launches, short reads. Handed-over timers were checked, not trusted: chat 04's cancel list arrived by message and its install claim was verified against the three Modern DLL ProductVersions on disk. A per-file PowerShell listing of ApplicationPlugins prints every backup too (about 100 lines): filter to the two live `.bundle` folders next time. Watchdog notice on 30 Sep 07:20 not yet proven: record whether it arrived.

12. 29 Sep evening (controller 06): two of my first four builders came back "already fixed" (BUG-198/199 in 89ad7fc2, BUG-195/196 in ccd31d62), both verified by ancestry and code. The carpark was stale because chat 04 fixed X1's findings on the release branch after Codex filed them on 3.0.14/1.0.85. BEFORE dispatching a builder for a carpark bug, check `git log origin/<live line> --grep=BUG-NNN` and the REGRESSION_LOG first (one command, saves a builder run); the bug goes to Part Z, not to a builder.

13. 30 Sep 07:27 (controller 06): the session-only timers (CronList showed all of them, including the 00:16 check, the 06:03 read, the 06:20 counting job, the 07:05 facts and the every-2-hours heartbeat) did NOT fire overnight; the controller was idle from 00:48 to 07:30 and woke only because the desk chat sent a message. The 23:47 launch one-shot had also not fired. RULE: never rely on a timer to start or check anything that matters; use the watchdog notice and the desk's WAKE message, and after every burst of work leave a running agent, a Monitor or a background command so a completion notification wakes the session. Lost about 6.5 hours of building and a queue of Opus jobs; the Claude meter sat at 3% against a pace of about 10%. (The watchdog's 07:20 notice had not arrived by 07:30: not yet proven.)
14. 30 Sep 07:27 (controller 06): a deep Codex AutoCAD pass on the installed build cost 19 Codex points (pass18: 56.0% -> 75.0%), not the 8 I had estimated from earlier passes: pass18 carried the UX judgement block and 103 checks. Budget by the meter, not by the old estimate: each deep part may cost 10-20 points; at 81% (07:27) sweep C1 has about 9 points before the 90% stop line.

### For Tim when he is around
1. **ANSWERED 29 Sep (plan/05, Tim ticked it): build the pick window - queue A5.** **BUG-160 (typed bottom level on a grade-held pipe).** Your rule: see the typed and automatic numbers and pick. A grip drag cannot stop mid-drag to ask, so the choice would come the moment you let go: a small window "Keep your 10.30 / Use automatic 10.25". Recommendation: build that window (Codex) and until then change nothing (today's behaviour stays). Alternative: a stop-gap that keeps your typed number and prints a line (you overruled this on 23 Sep, so not done without your word).
3. **ANSWERED 29 Sep (plan/05, Tim ticked it): "Unknown" is right.** **Pit Schedule FROM column (BUG-067):** where CE genuinely cannot tell where a pipe comes from, the schedule now prints "Unknown" instead of a blank. OK on a plotted drawing, or would you rather a dash "-" or blank? Recommendation: "Unknown" while drafting so it gets fixed before issue.
4. **ANSWERED 29 Sep (plan/05, Tim ticked it): keep the imported value, flag in Project Review - queue A6.** **XCIVIL imported pipes (BUG-065):** pipes now stop at the pit wall like CD pipes, but their levels still come from the pit's level text (often the pit-centre value). Should CE re-work the level at the wall from the grade, or keep the imported value? Recommendation: keep the imported value and flag it in Project Review, so nothing changes silently.
5. **Structural schedule floors (BUG-195 fix):** pergola beams (PB1) used to land on the Roof schedule only by accident of the old rule; they are kept on Roof for now. Right place, or their own group? Also a leading number like 3FB1 is read as a floor (Third), not a unit (unit 3) - fine, or should the job's "Tag prefix by: level / unit" setting decide?
6. **ANSWERED 29 Sep (plan/05, Tim ticked it): Structural wakes by itself** on any Structural command in a Civil drawing, no Yes/No question; CSWAKE by hand. Stands as built.
2. **Structural backup of Civil-only drawings** is now OFF by default (Civil keeps its own backup); tick "Also autosave Civil-only drawings" in Structural Settings to restore. Confirm you are happy with off.

Tim allowed the plan chat to post into the test session; it is not needed while the deploy chat drives. **Log of messages this chat posted to Codex: none.**

**Sandbox: ABANDONED, 23 Sep about 13:00 (deploy chat's proof).** Codex got AutoCAD running with CE loaded in the sandbox, and every CE write still went to Tim's real folders: CE finds its folders with `Environment.GetFolderPath(SpecialFolder.ApplicationData)`, which ignores the APPDATA redirection. A sandbox that looks isolated but is not is worse than none. (Also seen: the CUIX failed to load in the sandbox, so no CE ribbon; a WebView2 notice; a licence prompt on close, where Codex typed nothing.) **Codex pass 1 runs on Tim's live AutoCAD from 13:00** under rules 13-14. **Between-runs checks now also include `%APPDATA%\TIM_CE_CADTOOLS\CD_Settings.json`** (Tim's real CE settings; a hashed backup is held by the deploy chat and the whole file is put back Monday evening). The `CE_SANDBOX` registry profile is being exported and removed.

**Codex set-up (23 Sep, release chat's call):** the `C:\Autodesk\CERef\*-isolate` folders are headless-only (no plugins folder), so rule 14's isolated set-up does not exist yet. The deploy chat builds and proves a GUI sandbox first (redirected APPDATA with a plugins copy, a separate `/p` profile). **If it is not proven by 18:00 on 23 Sep, Codex runs on Tim's live AutoCAD** under rules 13-14, after a snapshot of his AutoCAD registry profile and support folder, because Tim asked for Codex testing over and over until Tuesday.

**Sandbox result and rule (23 Sep, about 12:50):** the deploy chat's first sandbox start did not load CE (AutoCAD likely stopped on a first-run dialog); nothing reached Tim's files (his CE log and `acadiso.dwt` unchanged). Attempt 2 clones his AutoCAD support folders into the sandbox. **Release chat's call:** file isolation is enough; the registry (`HKCU\Software\Autodesk\AutoCAD\R26.0`) is shared, so it is exported before and after every Codex run and diffed, with OSMODE watched (CE sets 512 during the CDWT callout pick). **Monday evening:** diff against the baseline `ACAD_R26_BEFORE_SANDBOX.reg`, put back values Codex changed, list them on Tim's Tuesday page. **Between every Codex run:** ~~read `OSMODE` under `<<Unnamed Profile>>` (4133) and put it back~~ **WITHDRAWN 23 Sep about 14:15: DO NOT write 4133 anywhere.** Read inside AutoCAD, Tim's live profile is `CPROFILE = "Tim"` with **OSMODE 21759**. There is no profile named Tim in HKCU (only `<<Unnamed Profile>>`, which holds the stale 4133), so the registry check measured the wrong thing. Writing 4133 would have replaced his snap set. The between-runs check is now `CPROFILE` and `OSMODE` read **inside AutoCAD** at the start and end of every Codex run (`PROFILE_CHECK_START`/`END`). The before-value is 21759. Nothing is written to the registry on Monday for OSMODE. Single named values only, never a blanket import. **`CD_Settings.json` on Monday, compared 23 Sep 14:55 against `CD_Settings_BEFORE_TESTING.json` (10:38):** two differences. (1) `debugLogging` is now `true`: Codex's S4 turned CDDEBUG on at about 13:49, with the deploy chat's exception. **Put it back to `false`** (or have Codex type `CDDEBUG Off` at the end of the last run), that one value only. (2) A new empty `waterTank.planForTank` entry, written by the new CDWT plan memory (CDWT-LINK-01). It is the plugin's own and harmless, so **leave it**. Also check that `\Profiles` `(default)` still reads `<<Unnamed Profile>>`: if a sandbox start with `/p` moved that pointer, Tim's next normal start would open in the sandbox profile. **Demonstrated 23 Sep, sandbox attempt 2: `/p CE_SANDBOX` DID move the pointer; the deploy chat put it back at once and verified it. This check is mandatory after every sandbox run.** (Attempts 1 and 2 did not load CE: AutoCAD stalled; Tim's CE log and `acadiso.dwt` stayed untouched through three starts.) **Registry writes made for testing, to list on Tuesday:** a copy of Tim's profile as `\Profiles\CE_SANDBOX` (deploy chat, 23 Sep, additive; exports held); removed after testing unless Tim keeps it. ## 4. The risks that decide the plan

1. **The shared common library.** Every CE plugin carries the same library and the first copy AutoCAD loads wins for all of them. A colleague given Civil 3.0 while keeping Manager 1.0.2 and Structural 1.0.41 (library 1.0.0) risks the CE ribbon silently not loading. **All three must move together, carrying the same library.**
2. **Mixed versions on one job.** Version 3 upgrades pipes into its own pipe object when a drawing is opened or drawn in. A colleague still on 2.1.14 opening that drawing has no enabler for that object. What they see is **not yet established** — probe first, then decide.
3. **AutoCAD 2022.** Colleagues run 2022 and 2027 (Tim, 23 Sep). The 2022 to 2024 lane of Civil 3.0 has no recorded AutoCAD check (PENDING_TESTS rows 18 and 19: "2024, never tested"), and this PC has AutoCAD 2027 only. Tim's Structural 1.0.74 has no 2022 lane at all.
4. **Known hazards on the installed build.** The CDWT crash (W4) has no established cause. The CD window layout fixes are not in it.
5. **The release script.** `build_installer.ps1` was broken until DEPLOY-01 (23 Sep) and still does not build the transform module (`CE_TRANSFORM_2027.arx`); it is built by hand.

### REL-09 How the hand install works — PROPOSED, read from the files 23 Sep

**What exists, read today:**

1. **The three install files in the IT Admin folder** (`Install-CECvlSuite.ps1`, `Install-CESuiteManager.ps1`, `Install-CEStrucSuite.ps1`) **delete the old plugin folder, then copy the new one. No backup, and no check that AutoCAD is closed.** Not safe for a pilot on their own.
2. **The new Civil installer program is not a double-click installer any more.** Since RS-01B2 it only activates an update that Manager has already staged (it needs `--stage-record`, `--target`, `--rollback-root`, `--result-root`). It does refuse while `acad` or `accoreconsole` is running and keeps a rollback copy, but a colleague cannot run it by hand.
3. **A folder in the plugins folder whose name ends in `.backup` is not loaded by AutoCAD.** Observed, not documented: Tim's own `%APPDATA%\Autodesk\ApplicationPlugins` holds 19 such folders (counted 23 Sep) and his AutoCAD loads normally.

**Proposed steps — no new script, every step visible:**

1. **Close AutoCAD.** Check Task Manager: no `acad.exe`, no `accoreconsole.exe`.
2. Open File Explorer at `%APPDATA%\Autodesk\ApplicationPlugins`.
3. **Rename** `CE_CVL_SUITE.bundle`, `CE_SUITE_MANAGER.bundle` and `CE_STRUC_SUITE.bundle` by adding `_PRE_V3.backup` to the end.
4. **Check `C:\ProgramData\Autodesk\ApplicationPlugins`** for a CE `.bundle` folder **with a live `PackageContents.xml`**. If one is there, stop and tell Tim: an old July Manager there carries common library 1.0.0 and would load first, and the ribbon can fail silently. A folder whose `PackageContents.xml` is renamed or missing is harmless: note it and carry on (deploy chat, 23 Sep). (Tim's own PC has an old Manager 1.0.0 there, switched off on 17 Jul by renaming its `PackageContents.xml` to `PackageContents.xml.DISABLED-duplicate-appdata-wins`; a colleague may have one that is still live.)
5. **Copy the three new `.bundle` folders** from the pilot folder into `%APPDATA%\Autodesk\ApplicationPlugins`.
6. Open AutoCAD. The CE ribbon loads. `CEMANAGER` shows the three new versions.

**Going back (one step each):** close AutoCAD; delete the three new folders; remove `_PRE_V3.backup` from the three old names; open AutoCAD.

**Still open:**

1. **Settings.** Version 3 may rewrite the colleague's CE settings files (`%APPDATA%\TIM_CE_CADTOOLS`, `%APPDATA%\CE_CVL_SUITE`). Whether 2.1.14 still reads them after going back is **not established**. Proposed: copy those two folders to a `_PRE_V3` copy at step 3 as well.
2. **Where the pilot package sits.** Putting it in the shared IT Admin folder is publishing and needs Tim's word; a separate `CE V3 PILOT` subfolder keeps the 2.1.14 copies other colleagues use untouched.
3. **Rehearsal (changed 23 Sep):** Friday's install on Tim's PC uses **exactly these steps**, then the going-back steps, then the install again, so both directions are proven in real AutoCAD before Tuesday. A spare Windows user profile was the first idea; creating a Windows account is not something a chat does, so it is dropped.

It goes onto Tim's PC only after Codex pass 2 finishes, so it does not change the build under test. Decided by this chat, 23 Sep, as the deployment controller.

> 1. Open File Explorer and paste `%APPDATA%\Autodesk\ApplicationPlugins` into the address bar.
> 2. Rename `CE_CVL_SUITE.bundle`, `CE_SUITE_MANAGER.bundle` and `CE_STRUC_SUITE.bundle` by adding `_PRE_V3.backup` to the end of each name. That is your way back.
> 3. Copy the three new `.bundle` folders from the pilot folder into this one.
> 4. Open AutoCAD. The CE ribbon tab should appear. Type `CEMANAGER` and check it shows Civil 3.0.0.
>
> **What is new** (only what Codex has passed in AutoCAD, as of pass 1, 23 Sep; pass 2 adds to this):
> 1. **AutoCAD no longer crashes when you close it** (four clean closes).
> 2. **CDWT finds the right tank plan for you:** pick a size and the matching plans come first, or it says there are none (B-T1, T2, T4, T6).
> 3. **Pit labels move with the pit:** resize a pit or move its label corner, and the number stays in its circle; one Undo puts it back (B-P1 to P3).
> 4. **CDLPD writes the council's full note** for Council, Kerb and a blank answer (B-D2, B-D4).
> 5. **CDFIXTAGS** reports every doubled source name with a reason and a next step (B-L12, L13).
> [PENDING pass 2: SBEAM steel beams, the Manager window, the pit batch editor, double-click to edit a level, CDDESIGN finding the council, the CDCALC criteria table.]
>
> **Please do not use yet** [FILL from Codex failures]. It is harmless and the CE menu still works]; [CDWT tank placement near pasted PDF pictures, pending BUG-163].
>
> **If something goes wrong.** Note what you clicked and what you expected. Send Tim the newest files from `%APPDATA%\CE_CVL_SUITE\logs`. Nothing in your drawings is lost by going back.
>
> **Going back to your July version (2 minutes).** Close AutoCAD. Delete the three new `.bundle` folders. Remove `_PRE_V3.backup` from the three old names. Open AutoCAD.

261. **27 Sep 01:25: Tim's working-style ruling (27 Sep ~00:30):** "lets be more efficient with usage and speed, using models that make sense, have a structure that can handle the workflow whilst getting things moving in a more timely matter, doing larger batches of fixes, tests and build items so we can feel and achieve more in less time. lets try reduce waste and be as efficient as possible". **How the release chat now works (from Sunday):** (1) ONE batch cycle per day - morning: all open fixes/builds briefed at once to parallel Sonnet builders (3-4, isolated worktrees, each runs its own tests); midday: ONE Opus review of the whole batch; afternoon: ONE train per product, ONE full suite each, ONE install; evening: ONE Codex AutoCAD pass covering everything new. No per-fix trains or one-off reviews. (2) Models: Sonnet for building and fixing; Opus only for the single batch review and proven-hard root causes (Undo, C7, data corruption); Codex sol-medium for builds, astra-low for AutoCAD tests. (3) The main chat coordinates only - no hand-fixing, no repeated focused test loops; plan/carpark updated once per batch, not per event. (4) Waste guards: every Structural test run uses --blame-hang-timeout; the 4-hour Structural suite once per train, overnight; Codex jobs timed to its 09:00 share step. **UX-NAV-01..05 (CE_PLAN f188c02, planning only):** a CE find box on the CE panel plus CEFIND (type P7, WT1 or a beam mark: zoom + highlight); clicking a schedule, Checks or Properties row zooms and highlights about 2 s with Back to the previous view; up/down arrows in Civil Properties walk the network pit, pipe, pit; right-click "Select this run" / "Select whole network"; a moving guide while walking (flow dashes running downstream on the current pipe, a slow breathing ring on the next stop, a fade-plus-tick trail on items passed; also on Checks jumps; screen-only, fades about 3 s after walking stops, slow pulse never strobing, Settings off switch) - a narrow, Tim-requested exception to the "no motion" cockpit rule.

Core console on the fixed build: correct beams follow as before, swapped beams stay put; Tim's live log and settings unchanged before/after. DesignCore focused 490/490 (3 frameworks), full net10 = baseline 6; Structural focused 94/94; 3 lanes 0/0; trial merge with #270 277/277. BUG-184 set IN PROGRESS. Only these windows and their supporting view models/tests changed; Settings and other builders' protected files untouched.

These windows retain loose local resources and call the existing MergeEditorShellResources before InitializeComponent. Save must finish an in-progress cell edit so invalid text cannot silently save the old number. Tim, midnight 24 September: "I am happy to scroll in places when you would have to implement a next page button like in the help window i just dont want high use windows to have scrolling areas where the ui and ux could be designed far better". Layout remains fixed; only bounded lists and label text scroll.
